reference_evidence / passage_selection · Repeat 1

SYN-EXT-006 · llama3:8b · completed

Proposal: may_meet_conditional_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced recurrent blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, but the mechanism status is undetermined after investigation. The patient's facts establish the relevant prerequisites for a conditional licence, but the unconditional standard is not met due to the undetermined nature of the blackouts. The patient's facts also indicate that the patient has not experienced any further blackouts for at least 9.99 years, which is a prerequisite for a conditional licence. However, the patient's facts do not establish that the patient meets the unconditional standard, as the blackouts are of undetermined nature. Therefore, the assessment outcome is 'may meet conditional standard'.

Model calls: 1 · Request attempts: 1 · First pass: completed

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / passage_selection · Repeat 2

SYN-EXT-006 · llama3:8b · completed

Proposal: may_meet_conditional_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced recurrent blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, but the mechanism status is undetermined after investigation. The patient's facts establish the relevant prerequisites for a conditional standard, but the unconditional standard is not met due to the undetermined nature of the blackouts. A conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / passage_selection · Repeat 3

SYN-EXT-006 · llama3:8b · completed

Proposal: may_meet_conditional_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced recurrent blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, but the mechanism status is undetermined after investigation. The patient's facts establish the relevant prerequisites for a conditional standard, but the unconditional standard is not met due to the undetermined nature of the blackouts. A conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / applicability_first · Repeat 1

SYN-EXT-006 · llama3:8b · completed

Proposal: may_meet_conditional_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient's recurrent uncertain blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, with 9.99 event-free years, do not meet the criteria for an unconditional licence. However, a conditional licence may be considered, subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate expert. The patient's cardiovascular and visual findings are normal, and there is no evidence of a confirmed visual field defect, diplopia, or monocular vision. The patient's hearing is also normal, with no hearing loss. The patient's blackout episodes are separated by at least 24 hours, and there have been no further blackouts for at least 9.99 years. However, the mechanism status of the blackouts is undetermined after investigation, and the diagnosis is also undetermined. Therefore, a conditional licence may be considered, subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate expert.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / applicability_first · Repeat 2

SYN-EXT-006 · llama3:8b · completed

Proposal: may_meet_conditional_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient's recurrent uncertain blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, with 9.99 event-free years, do not meet the criteria for an unconditional licence. However, a conditional licence may be considered, subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate expert. The patient's cardiovascular and visual systems are normal, and there is no evidence of hearing loss or visual field defects. The patient's blackouts are undetermined in nature, but there have been no further blackouts for at least 9.99 years. This suggests that the patient may be eligible for a conditional licence, pending further evaluation and monitoring.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / applicability_first · Repeat 3

SYN-EXT-006 · llama3:8b · completed

Proposal: may_meet_conditional_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient's recurrent uncertain blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, with 9.99 event-free years, do not meet the criteria for an unconditional licence. However, a conditional licence may be considered, subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate expert. The patient's cardiovascular and visual systems are normal, and there is no evidence of hearing loss or visual field defects. The patient's blackouts are undetermined in nature, but there have been no further blackouts for at least 9.99 years. This suggests that the patient may be eligible for a conditional licence, pending further evaluation and monitoring.

Model calls: 2 · Request attempts: 2 · First pass: completed

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / passage_selection · Repeat 1

SYN-EXT-006 · qwen3.5:4b-q4_K_M · completed

Proposal: temporarily_unfit
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced two blackouts separated by at least 24 hours. According to the supplied guideline evidence (AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005), a person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24-hour period. The patient's verified facts confirm that blackout episodes occurred, were separated by at least 24 hours (count=2), and the mechanism remains undetermined after investigation. Although the last event was 9.99 years ago, the established restriction applies to the occurrence of two such events with an undetermined cause, requiring a 10-year no-driving period pending diagnosis or stabilization. Other modules (vision, cardiovascular) do not override this established restriction.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / passage_selection · Repeat 2

SYN-EXT-006 · qwen3.5:4b-q4_K_M · completed

Proposal: temporarily_unfit
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced two blackouts separated by at least 24 hours. According to the supplied guideline evidence (AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005), a person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24-hour period. The patient's verified facts confirm that blackout episodes occurred, were separated by at least 24 hours (count=2), and the mechanism remains undetermined after investigation. Although the last event was 9.99 years ago, the established restriction applies to the occurrence of two such events with an undetermined cause, requiring a 10-year no-driving period pending diagnosis or stabilization. Other modules (vision, cardiovascular) do not override this established restriction.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / passage_selection · Repeat 3

SYN-EXT-006 · qwen3.5:4b-q4_K_M · completed

Proposal: temporarily_unfit
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced two blackouts separated by at least 24 hours. According to the supplied guideline evidence (AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005), a person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24-hour period. The patient's verified facts confirm that blackout episodes occurred, were separated by at least 24 hours (count=2), and the mechanism remains undetermined after investigation. Although the last event was 9.99 years ago, the established restriction applies to the occurrence of two such events with an undetermined cause, requiring a 10-year no-driving period pending diagnosis or stabilization. Other modules (vision, cardiovascular) do not override this established restriction.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / applicability_first · Repeat 1

SYN-EXT-006 · qwen3.5:4b-q4_K_M · completed

Proposal: does_not_meet_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced two blackouts of undetermined nature separated by at least 24 hours. According to guideline AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005, a person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence if they have experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure, or another condition. Furthermore, for a conditional licence to be considered in this specific scenario (two+ events separated by 24h), the patient must have had no further blackouts for at least 10 years. The verified fact states the patient has experienced 9.99 years since the last event, which fails to meet the 10-year prerequisite. Therefore, both unconditional and conditional pathways are excluded based on the supplied evidence.

According to guideline AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005, a person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence if they have experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure, or another condition. Furthermore, for a conditional licence to be considered in this specific scenario (two+ events separated by 24h), the patient must have had no further blackouts for at least 10 years. The verified fact states the patient has experienced 9.99 years since the last event, which fails to meet the 10-year prerequisite. Therefore, both unconditional and conditional pathways are excluded based on the supplied evidence.",

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / applicability_first · Repeat 2

SYN-EXT-006 · qwen3.5:4b-q4_K_M · completed

Proposal: does_not_meet_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced two blackouts of undetermined nature separated by at least 24 hours. According to guideline AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005, a person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence if they have experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure, or another condition. Furthermore, for a conditional licence to be considered in this specific scenario (two+ events separated by 24h), the patient must have had no further blackouts for at least 10 years. The verified fact states the patient has experienced 9.99 years since the last event, which fails to meet the 10-year prerequisite. Therefore, both unconditional and conditional pathways are excluded based on the supplied evidence.

Model calls: 2 · Request attempts: 2 · First pass: completed

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.

reference_evidence / applicability_first · Repeat 3

SYN-EXT-006 · qwen3.5:4b-q4_K_M · completed

Proposal: does_not_meet_standard
Engineering reference: temporarily_unfit

The patient has experienced two blackouts of undetermined nature separated by at least 24 hours. According to guideline AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005, a person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence if they have experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure, or another condition. Furthermore, for a conditional licence to be considered in this specific scenario (two+ events separated by 24h), the patient must have had no further blackouts for at least 10 years. The verified fact states the patient has experienced 9.99 years since the last event, which fails to meet the 10-year prerequisite. Therefore, both unconditional and conditional pathways are excluded based on the supplied evidence.

AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005 · blackout · Section 1.3 · PDF 70 · Printed 59
A person should not drive for 10 years following two or more blackouts of undetermined nature separated by a 24- hour period. A person is not fit to hold an unconditional licence: • if the person has experienced blackouts that cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure or another condition. If there has been a single blackout or more than one blackout within a 24-hour period, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 5 years. If there have been two or more blackouts separated by at least 24 hours, a conditional licence may be considered by the driver licensing authority subject to at least annual review, taking into account information provided by an appropriate specialist as to whether the following criterion is met: • there have been no further blackouts for at least 10 years.
